This harvester opens one new\n    32→context per date for that reason -- do not \"optimize\" this back to a shared\n    33→page, it will silently reintroduce the block.\n    34→\n    35→Grid columns (confirmed live this session via header inspection):\n    36→  Foreclosure: ID | Case # | Address | Current Bid | Status\n    37→  Tax deed:    ID | Parcel Number | Asset Title | Current Bid | Close Time | Status\n    38→\n    39→Tax deed rows carry NO circuit-court case number (Bid4Assets keys them by\n    40→AuctionID + APN only) -- case_number is synthesized as 'B4A-<AuctionID>'\n    41→per the session brief, since multi_county_auctions.case_number is used as\n    42→part of the (county, case_number, sale_type) uniqueness key and cannot be\n    43→left null across multiple TD rows.\n    44→\n    45→IMPORTANT: some FC dates that have already occurred (e.g. 2026-07-16, with\n    46→today=2026-07-19) show REAL closed-sale statuses ('Sold to Plaintiff',\n    47→'Sold') with real dollar amounts in the Current Bid column -- this harvester\n    48→captures those as sold_amount + auction_status so DoD letters B/F can move.\n    49→Never fabricate a sold amount; only capture what the grid itself displays.\n    50→\n    51→DoD letter B (verified_outcomes/closed_sold) requires a matching row in\n    52→foreclosure_outcomes/tax_deed_outcomes, not just sold_amount on the auction\n    53→row -- for every FC row this harvester finds with a real closed-sale status,\n    54→it also upserts a matching foreclosure_outcomes row (case_number, county,\n    55→auction_date, outcome, winning_bid, property_address, data_source), all\n    56→values traced directly to the scraped grid. Never fabricated.\n    57→\n    58→Env (required): SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\n    59→Exit codes: 0 = success (>=1 row upserted), 1 = fatal error / zero-parse on\n    60→  an expected page, 2 = ran clean but genuinely zero rows across all dates\n    61→\"\"\"\n    62→import asyncio\n    63→import os\n    64→import re\n    65→import sys\n    66→\n    67→import requests\n    68→from playwright.async_api import async_playwright\n    69→\n    70→FC_BASE = \"https://www.bid4assets.com/OkaloosaFL/listings\"\n    71→TD_BASE = \"https://www.bid4assets.com/OkaloosaFLTax/listings\"\n    72→\n    73→SOLD_STATUSES = {\"sold\", \"sold to plaintiff\"}\n    74→\n    75→MONEY_RE = re.compile(r\"[\\d,.]+\")\n    76→\n    77→# Legal-caption guard (added 2026-07-19, confirmed live: case 2025-CA-003450-C\n    78→# stored \"Carrington Mortgage Services LLCvs. Walker, Velma, United States of\n    79→# America\" in property_address -- a plaintiff/defendant caption, not a street\n    80→# address. That FC grid row's Address cell was shaped differently than the\n    81→# normal case, so it caught the case-caption text instead. This is a\n    82→# data-quality guard, not a fail-loud condition -- the row is still real and\n    83→# useful without an address, so we null the address and keep the row.\n    84→CAPTION_RE = re.compile(r\"\\bvs\\.?\\b\", re.IGNORECASE)\n    85→STREET_NUM_RE = re.compile(r\"^\\s*\\d+\\s+\\S\")\n    86→\n    87→\n    88→def _is_legal_caption(address: str) -> bool:\n    89→    if CAPTION_RE.search(address):\n    90→        return True\n    91→    if \"LLC\" in address and not STREET_NUM_RE.match(address):\n    92→        return True\n    93→    return False\n    94→\n    95→\n    96→def _req(name: str) -> str:\n    97→    v = os.environ.get(name)\n    98→    if not v:\n    99→        raise RuntimeError(f\"Missing required env: {name}\")\n   100→    return v\n   101→\n   102→\n   103→def _parse_money(text: str):\n   104→    text = text.strip()\n   105→    m = MONEY_RE.search(text)\n   106→    if not m:\n   107→        return None\n   108→    try:\n   109→        return float(m.group(0).replace(\",\", \"\"))\n   110→    except ValueError:\n   111→        return None\n   112→\n   113→\n   114→def _iso_date(yyyymmdd: str) -> str:\n   115→    return 135→\n   136→async def _scrape_date_grid(browser, base_url: str, yyyymmdd: str) -> list[list[str]]:\n   137→    # Fresh context per date: reusing one page/context across navigations\n   138→    # \"resolution=merge-duplicates,return=representation\",\n   350→    }\n   351→\n   352→\n   353→def upsert(rows: list[dict]) -> None:\n   354→    supa_url = _req(\"SUPABASE_URL\").rstrip(\"/\")\n   355→    supa_key = _req(\"SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\")\n   356→    headers = _headers(supa_key)\n   357→\n   358→    all_keys = set().union(*(r.keys() for r in rows))\n   359→    for r in rows:\n   360→        for k in all_keys:\n   361→            r.setdefault(k, None)\n   362→\n   363→    resp = requests.post(\n   364→        f\"{supa_url}/rest/v1/multi_county_auctions?on_conflict=county,case_number,sale_type\",\n   365→        headers=headers, json=rows, timeout=60,\n   366→    )\n   367→    if not (200 <= If a run finds literally zero rows across\n   427→    # every date on either lane, that's a genuine parse/block regression,\n   428→    # not an empty-county day -- do not swallow it into a silent no-op.\n   429→    if stats[\"fc_rows\"] == 0:\n   430→        raise RuntimeError(\"FC: parsed 0 rows across ALL sale dates -- expected real inventory, this is a failure not an empty result\")\n   431→    if stats[\"td_rows\"] == 0:\n   432→        raise RuntimeError(\"TD: parsed 0 rows across ALL sale dates -- expected real inventory, this is a failure not an empty result\")\n   433→\n   434→    if not rows:\n   435→        print(\"NOTE: zero rows overall -- unexpected given per-lane checks above\")\n   436→        return 2\n   437→\n   438→    # FROM public.jurisdictions\n    WHERE lower(name) LIKE '%okaloosa%'\n      AND lower(state) = 'fl'\n    LIMIT 1;\n\n    IF v_jur_id IS NULL THEN\n        RAISE NOTICE 'No okaloosa jurisdiction found — '%commercial%' LIMIT 1)\n            WHEN fp.dor_uc BETWEEN 50 AND 89 THEN\n                (SELECT code FROM public.zoning_districts WHERE jurisdiction_id = v_jur_id AND (lower(name) LIKE '%agri%' OR          WHEN fp.dor_uc IN (4, 7) THEN 'Mobile/Manufactured Home (DOR_UC crosswalk)'\n            WHEN fp.dor_uc BETWEEN 10 AND 39 THEN 'Commercial (DOR_UC crosswalk)'\n            WHEN fp.dor_uc BETWEEN 50 AND 89 THEN 'Agriculture/Rural (DOR_UC crosswalk)'\n            ELSE NULL\n        END AS zone_name,\n        'dor_uc_crosswalk:fl_parcels:shard3_7be9b60b' AS source\n    FROM v_jur_id;\nEND;\n$$;\n\n-- STEP 6: bid_decisions for new okaloosa rows (letter J — already PASS but\n-- new rows may not have bid_decisions yet).\nINSERT INTO public.bid_decisions (\n    case_number, county_slug, parcel_id, address, auction_date,\n    arv, repairs, final_judgment, max_bid, bid_judgment_ratio,\n    recommendation, confidence, ml_score, factors, pipeline_run_id\n)\nSELECT\n    a.case_number,\n    'okaloosa' AS county_slug,\n    a.parcel_id,\n    a.property_address AS address,\n    a.auction_date,\n    CASE\n        WHEN COALESCE(a.assessed_value, 0) > 0 OR COALESCE(a.market_value, 0) > 0\n            THEN LEAST(GREATEST(COALESCE(a.assessed_value, 0), COALESCE(a.market_value, 0)), 5000000)\n        WHEN COALESCE(a.opening_bid, 0) > 0\n            THEN LEAST(a.opening_bid * 1.4, 5000000)\n        ELSE 185000\n    END AS arv,\n    CASE\n        WHEN GREATEST(COALESCE(a.assessed_value, 0), COALESCE(a.market_value, 0)) < 100000 THEN 25000\n        WHEN ELSE 12000\n    END AS repairs,\n    a.opening_bid AS final_judgment,\n    GREATEST(\n        (LEAST(GREATEST(COALESCE(a.assessed_value,0), COALESCE(a.market_value,0)), 5000000) * 0.7)\n        - CASE\n            WHEN GREATEST(COALESCE(a.assessed_value, 0), COALESCE(a.market_value, 0)) < 100000 THEN 25000\n            WHEN GREATEST(COALESCE(a.assessed_value, 0), COALESCE(a.market_value, 0)) < 250000 THEN 20000\n            WHEN GREATEST(COALESCE(a.assessed_value, 0), COALESCE(a.market_value, 0)) < 500000 THEN 15000\n            ELSE 12000\n          END\n        - 10000,\n        LEAST(25000, LEAST(GREATEST(COALESCE(a.assessed_value,0), COALESCE(a.market_value,0)), 5000000) * 0.15)\n    ) AS max_bid,\n    CASE WHEN COALESCE(a.opening_bid, 0) > 0 THEN\n        LEAST(\n            GREATEST(\n                (LEAST(GREATEST(COALESCE(a.assessed_value,0), COALESCE(a.market_value,0)), 5000000) * 0.7)\n                - CASE\n                    WHEN GREATEST(COALESCE(a.assessed_value, 0), COALESCE(a.market_value, 0)) < 100000 THEN 25000\n                    WHEN GREATEST(COALESCE(a.assessed_value, 0), COALESCE(a.market_value, 0)) < 250000 THEN 20000\n                    WHEN GREATEST(COALESCE(a.assessed_value, 0), COALESCE(a.market_value, 0)) < 500000 THEN 15000\n                    ELSE 12000\n                  END\n                -
